I don't know, it's like I'm making up for the whole day or something. \n\nSo... yeah. That's what brought me in.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're able to stop eating once you start, or do you feel like you can't control it?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. It's hard to explain. Once I start on those snacks... I just keep telling myself to close the bag, you know? Just put it away and go to bed. But... my hands just keep reaching for more. I don't stop until... well, until I feel embarrassed. And just kind of sick of myself.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty after these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know. I guess I haven't really thought about it like that. I just... I don't feel great about it, obviously. But I mostly just try to go to sleep and... not dwell on it. I'm not really sure.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever eat a large amount of food in a short period of time, like within an hour or two?\n\nPatient: I... I don't really know. I never really timed it. It just sort of happens over the evening, I guess. I haven't really paid attention to if it's a huge amount all at once, or... or how many hours it takes.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in control of your eating during these episodes?\n\nPatient: I... I think I mentioned this a minute ago. But, no. Like I said, I tell myself to just put the bag away and go to sleep, but... I don't. My hands just keep going for more. I can't seem to stop until I just feel... sick of myself. So... no, I guess I don't feel in control.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever try to make up for the calories you consumed during these late-night eating episodes by doing something like exercising, vomiting, or using laxatives?\n\nPatient: I... well. I don't really like to talk about that. But... yeah, a few times, I guess. After those late-night spells. I've... gone to the bathroom and... made myself throw up. I just... I was scared all that food was going to show on me. But it's only been a few times.\n\nDoctor: How often do these late-night eating episodes happen?\n\nPatient: I... well. Probably about once a week. It's been going on for the last three months or so. It mostly happens on the nights after my evening class... I'll get home and overeat like we talked about, and then... make myself sick afterward. Just... about once a week.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're eating much more rapidly than usual during these episodes?\n\nPatient: I... I'm not really sure. I haven't noticed if I eat faster. I don't think so. Just... normal speed, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you eat much more than you normally would during these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: I... yeah, I suppose I do. It's... well, after that class, I'll stop and get a whole fast-food meal. And... maybe some candy from the vending machine. Then I get home and just... keep having more snacks. I don't know, it's like... like I'm making up for the whole day, I guess. So... yeah, it's a lot.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty or ashamed after these episodes of eating at night?\n\nPatient: I... no, I wouldn't really say that. I don't really dwell on it. I just... clean up and go to bed.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you've eaten an amount of food that's definitely larger than what most people would eat under similar circumstances during these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: I mean... I guess so. Like I said before, it's... it's a full meal, then the candy, then the snacks at home. I don't know what other people eat, but... yeah, I suppose it's a lot.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up after one of these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: I... well, it's been happening for... maybe the last three months or so. Usually... at least once a week. After that class, when I... when I eat like that. Then I... yeah, I make myself do it.\n\nDoctor: How often do you have episodes where you eat a large amount of food and feel like you can't stop, without making yourself throw up or using other ways to compensate for the calories afterwards?\n\nPatient: I... no. I don't really do that. If I end up eating like that, I... I always do what I just told you. I don't just... leave it.\n\nDoctor: How often do you have episodes where you eat a large amount of food and feel like you can't stop, without making yourself throw up afterwards?\n\nPatient: I... I think I just said that. Never. If I eat that much, I always... you know, do what I mentioned. I don't just leave it.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month do you have episodes where you eat a large amount of food and feel like you can't stop, without making yourself throw up afterwards?\n\nPatient: I... look, I already answered this. I don't... it's zero times a month. If I eat like that, I always... I don't let it stay down.\n\nDoctor: How many times a month do you have episodes where you eat a large amount of food and feel like you can't stop?\n\nPatient: Um... well, if you mean overall... I guess for about the past three months, it's usually been at least one night a week. Usually happens after my evening class. I'll... overeat like that, and then make myself throw up afterward. So... about four times a month, I suppose.\n\nDoctor: In the past three months, how many times have you eaten a large amount of food and felt like you couldn't stop, without then making yourself throw up?\n\nPatient: I... I really thought I made this clear already. None. Zero times. I don't... I don't ever do that without... getting rid of it.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
